# Foodie — Project Worklog

---
Task ID: 1
Agent: Z.ai Code (main orchestrator)
Task: Build "Foodie" v1 — Food Discovery foundation (Next.js 16, mobile-first, mock data)

Work Log:
- Inspected scaffold: Next.js 16 App Router + shadcn/ui + Tailwind 4 + Prisma (unused for v1 per requirements).
- Fetched 15 realistic food image sets (75+ images) via `z-ai image-search` CLI; learned to run calls sequentially with retries (parallel calls hit 429 rate limits) and that `-o` output is unreliable, so stdout was captured and parsed.
- Created domain layer:
  - `src/lib/foodie/types.ts` — Place, Dish, FoodCategory, EatContext, PriceRange, PlaceBadges (maps to future Supabase tables).
  - `src/lib/foodie/constants.ts` — 10 food categories, 5 eating-with contexts, mock current location (Koramangala, Bengaluru), app name/tagline.
  - `src/lib/foodie/store.ts` — Zustand store with state-based screen routing (splash → auth → onboarding → home → detail), preferences, search, category filter.
  - `src/lib/foodie/utils.ts` — open/closed hours logic (incl. past-midnight closing), search matcher, section sorters, INR formatting.
- Mock data: `src/data/places.ts` — 14 realistic Bengaluru places with badges (hiddenGem / localFavorite / popular / budget), famous + signature dishes with ₹ prices, hours, photos (image-search OSS URLs), local tips, Google Maps queries.
- Reusable components (`src/components/foodie/`): Logo (inline SVG + favicon `public/logo.svg`), SectionHeader, SearchBar, CategoryChip/CategoryTile, PlaceCards (CarouselCard / ListRow / WideCard / ranked), Badges (RatingBadge, PlaceBadge).
- Screens (`src/components/foodie/screens/`): SplashScreen (gradient + floating emojis + tagline + Get Started), AuthScreen (Sign Up / Log In tabs, mock auth), EatingWithContextScreen ("Who are you eating with?" — 5 options, auto-advance), PreferencesScreen (10 category tiles, multi-select), HomeScreen (location header, "What are you craving?" search, category chips, greeting card, Nearby / Hidden Gems / Local Favorites / Popular Places / Budget Eats sections with see-all toggles, coming-soon teaser), PlaceDetailScreen (photo carousel with dots + badges, rating, price range, location, open/closed hours, famous dishes, signature dishes, photos grid, local recommendation, sticky Get Directions → Google Maps).
- Shell: `FoodieApp.tsx` — mobile-first app surface (full-bleed on phones, rounded device mockup + desktop side-branding panel on sm+), AnimatePresence screen transitions.
- App wiring: `page.tsx` renders FoodieApp; `layout.tsx` with Outfit font + Foodie metadata + sonner Toaster; `globals.css` — orange brand theme (oklch), custom `@utility` classes (foodie-gradient, no-scrollbar, thin-scrollbar, foodie-dots, pb-safe).
- Bug fixes during verification:
  1. Tailwind v4 dropped `@layer utilities` custom classes → converted to `@utility` directives.
  2. Image URLs were missing file extensions → script re-mapped all 71 URLs to correct extensions (jpg/jpeg/png/webp).
  3. "Loved by Xk locals" math error on WideCard → ratingCount/1000.
  4. Nested `<main>` elements → outer wrapper became a div.
  5. Desktop side-panel wordmark invisible (dark tone on dark bg) → Logo tone="light".

Stage Summary:
- All 6 requested experiences are implemented and browser-verified: splash, login/signup, onboarding (eating-with), food preferences, home discovery feed, place detail page.
- Verified via agent-browser (390×844 + 1600×900): full signup flow, login flow, search ("dosa" → 1 result), category filter (Cafe → 3 results), see-all expand, carousel dots, open/closed hours logic (Momo Nation "Closed right now" at ~9:45 AM IST), Get Directions Google Maps URL, back navigation, toasts, desktop mockup layout, mobile full-bleed.
- `bun run lint` clean; dev server compiles with no errors; no console/page errors.
- Architecture is ready for Supabase (types mirror tables), real auth, location services, and AI recommendations without structural rewrites.

Task ID: 2
Agent: Z.ai Code (main orchestrator)
Task: Add REAL location feature — native Geolocation API, Haversine distances, distance filter + "Nearest First" sort, manual city fallback

Work Log:
- Inspected existing Foodie v1 (state-routed screens, mock places, Zustand store) before touching anything.
- `src/lib/foodie/types.ts`: added `GeoPosition {lat,lng}`, required `latitude`/`longitude` on `Place` (removed hardcoded `distanceKm`), added `PlaceWithDistance`.
- NEW `src/lib/foodie/location.ts` — reusable location module: `requestBrowserLocation()` (Promise wrapper over `navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition`, high accuracy, 12s timeout, friendly error mapping for denied/unavailable/timeout/unsupported), `haversineKm()` (6371km radius, single source of truth), `formatDistance()` ("0.8 km away", metres under 100m), `formatLatLng()`, `reverseGeocode()` (best-effort OSM Nominatim, 5s abort, null fallback), `withDistances()` (annotates any coordinate-bearing list), `PROTOTYPE_CITY` + 8 `MANUAL_CITIES` with real center coords.
- NEW `src/lib/foodie/locationStore.ts` — Zustand location state: status (idle/locating/granted/denied/error/unsupported), source (prototype/browser/manual), `origin` (effective distance origin), browser `coords` + accuracy, `areaLabel`, `requestLocation/selectCity/usePrototypeCity`, plus derived `locationHeadline` + memoized `useLocationHeadline()` (zustand v5 needs stable selector results — fixed a getSnapshot infinite-loop crash found in browser testing) and `originSourceLabel()`.
- `src/lib/foodie/store.ts`: added `distanceFilterKm` (null=any) + `sortBy` ("nearest"|"rating") with setters.
- `src/lib/foodie/utils.ts`: distance-aware sorters/filters (`PlaceWithDistance[]`), `constants.ts`: removed fake `CURRENT_LOCATION`.
- `src/data/places.ts`: all 14 places now carry realistic WGS-84 coords around Koramangala (spread 0.3–2.3 km incl. honest Bellandur outlier at 2.3 km).
- NEW `src/components/foodie/LocationControls.tsx`: `LocationPromptBanner` ("Use my current location" CTA, status-aware copy) + `LocationSheet` (status card per state, gradient GPS button, 8-city manual grid, prototype-city reset, Escape/backdrop close, in-frame so it stays inside the phone mockup).
- NEW `src/components/foodie/FilterBar.tsx`: distance chips (Any/Within 1/3/5/10 km) + custom in-frame sort popover ("Nearest First" / "Top Rated").
- `HomeScreen.tsx`: full rewiring — withDistances(origin) → distance filter → sort; all 5 sections respect the radius; empty-state ("No places within X km") + section hiding; location status header (live green dot, amber permission warning, manual label); search respects filter+sort.
- `PlaceCards.tsx`: cards render computed `formatDistance` (distance-first in list rows so it never truncates); `PlaceDetailScreen.tsx`: computed distance + "Distance from {origin}" line; Get Directions upgrades to `google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&destination=lat,lng` when a real GPS fix exists.
- Bug fix during verification: `useLocationStore(locationHeadline)` returned a fresh object per call → React getSnapshot infinite loop on Home; replaced with primitive subscriptions + useMemo via `useLocationHeadline()`. Verified zero console errors after fix.
- Browser-verified (agent-browser 390×844 + 1600×900): signup/login/onboarding → home; prototype distances match hand-computed Haversine (0.3/0.4/0.5 km); Within 1 km excludes the 6 far places; sort switches rating↔distance; REAL denied path (headless denies permission → amber "Location permission required", app keeps working); manual Mumbai → honest ~849 km distances; emulated granted fix (Indiranagar) → header "Using your current location / Hoysala Nagara Central, Bengaluru" (live reverse geocode), distances recompute (4.3–5.2 km), empty state at Within 1 km, detail "4.3 km away · Distance from your current location", directions URL uses coordinates; desktop frame + mobile full-bleed both hold.

Stage Summary:
- Location feature is REAL: native Geolocation API only, no hardcoded user coords, no IP fallback; origin = GPS fix > manual city > prototype city, always labelled honestly in the UI.
- All mock coords + stores structured for the future Supabase/places-API swap (`withDistances` works on any `{latitude, longitude}[]`).
- All 14 requirements met; existing screens unaffected (splash/auth/onboarding/prefs/detail all re-verified); lint clean; zero runtime errors.

Task ID: 3
Agent: Z.ai Code (main orchestrator)
Task: Supabase database foundation — real restaurant DB layer with labelled DEMO fallback (keep ALL location features intact)

Work Log:
- Installed @supabase/supabase-js@2.116.0; added NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL / NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY to .env (empty → demo mode) + documented .env.example; .env* already gitignored.
- SQL foundation (supabase/): schema.sql (restaurants/dishes/reviews/restaurant_photos with exact required columns, FKs with on delete cascade/set null, check constraints, btree indexes incl. (latitude, longitude), full RLS — public read, authenticated-only self writes — and a pure-SQL nearby_restaurants(lat,lng,radius_km) RPC doing Haversine IN Postgres + commented PostGIS upgrade path); seed-demo.sql (5 restaurants all prefixed "DEMO —", dishes with nutrition fields, review, photo); unseed-demo.sql (cleanup by prefix); README.md (project creation → schema → env → first real restaurant → nearby-search architecture diagram).
- Supabase client layer (src/lib/supabase/): config.ts (env validation, isSupabaseConfigured()), server.ts (singleton anon client for API routes, persistSession:false).
- Data layer (src/lib/foodie/data/): types.ts (row shapes mirroring schema.sql + DataMode + response types + notices); mappers.ts (row→Place with documented conventions: description→tagline, city→area, price_level→costForTwo brackets, is_signature→famous/signature split, popular=rating≥4.6, budget=₹; DEMO_ID_PREFIX "demo:" + uuid guard); demoBackend.ts (labelled fallback over src/data/places.ts, ids prefixed); supabaseBackend.ts (RPC-first nearby → bbox+Haversine-in-Node fallback; byId fetches restaurant+dishes+photos in parallel; sanitized .or ilike search; category eq); serverRepository.ts (single dispatch: configured→Supabase, unconfigured→demo, Supabase-error→demo+notice); repository.ts (THE UI-facing functions: getNearbyRestaurants(lat,lng,radiusKm), getRestaurantById, getRestaurantsByCategory, searchRestaurants via /api/restaurants/*); useRestaurantFeed.ts (keyed-outcome hook — loading/ready/error + auto-refetch on origin change; no sync setState in effects per react-hooks/set-state-in-effect).
- API routes (force-dynamic, no-store): /api/restaurants/nearby (lat/lng/radiusKm validation 400s), /[id] (404 unknown), /search (q required, 120-char cap), /category/[category] (validated against FOOD_CATEGORIES).
- UI rewiring: HomeScreen — PLACES import REMOVED, feed via useRestaurantFeed(origin); FeedSkeleton loading, FeedErrorCard with retry, EmptyDatabaseCard (distinguishes "Supabase connected but table empty" vs "demo doesn't cover this origin" — fixed misleading copy found in testing); DemoDataBanner (amber strip, tap → Supabase setup toast) when mode==="demo"; distance/filter/sort math unchanged on top of fetched data. PlaceDetailScreen — PLACES import REMOVED, getRestaurantById fetch with DetailSkeleton, upgraded "place moved its kitchen" state (retry + back), 🧪 Demo data chip, ratingCount>0 guards, empty-photos gradient hero, localTip/photos guards. PlaceCards — PlacePhoto placeholder component + WideCard ratingCount guard. src/data/places.ts header now screams "DEMO DATASET — NOT REAL BUSINESSES".
- Verification: bun run lint clean (fixed 2 set-state-in-effect errors via keyed-outcome pattern); curl: all 4 routes + demo notice + honest empty (Mumbai origin → 0 places), 404/400 paths; agent-browser E2E (390×844 + 1600×900): full flow splash→auth→onboarding→home, DEMO banner + tap-toast, GPS-denied path (amber, prototype fallback, app alive), manual Mumbai refetch with real Mumbai coords in request (19.076,72.8777), Bengaluru restore, detail via API (demo:dosa-corner → dishes/photos/distance/demo chip), search "dosa"→1 result, desktop frame intact, zero console errors. dev.log shows REAL user GPS fix (13.2886,77.5984) flowing into nearby query — granted path live for actual users. Resilience test with fake credentials: RPC fail → warn → bbox fail → demo+notice, exit 0.

Stage Summary:
- Foodie now has a complete Supabase database foundation: schema+RLS+RPC in SQL, official JS client, clean repository layer, 4 API routes — UI contains zero restaurant data.
- Nearby search uses DB-stored lat/lng (RPC in Postgres first, Haversine fallback), fed by the real browser GPS fix; old hardcoded coords are never the user location (prototype fallback remains labelled).
- No credentials configured → app runs in clearly-labelled DEMO DATA mode (banner + per-place chips + honest notices); crashes impossible on Supabase failure.
- All existing screens/features (splash, auth, onboarding, prefs, location sheet, filters, detail, directions) re-verified untouched.

Task ID: 9
Agent: Z.ai Code (main)
Task: Generate review-ready INSERT SQL for the 8 approved A-list places (execute NOTHING; invent nothing; idempotent ON CONFLICT).

Work Log:
- Schema conflict found: latitude/longitude/price_level were NOT NULL — incompatible with "uncertain → NULL" for 7 coord-less places. Part 0 of the new SQL drops those three NOT NULLs (idempotent, non-destructive); rating keeps NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 (= "not yet captured", UI hides badge at 0).
- Created supabase/insert-batch-001-pilot-8.sql: BEGIN/COMMIT atomic; 3 ALTERs; 8 INSERT…SELECT…WHERE NOT EXISTS(lower(btrim(name)), city)…ON CONFLICT (id) DO NOTHING using 8 pre-generated fixed UUIDs (uses existing PK — no new constraint); per-field source comments; final verification SELECT; commented UPDATE template for later coordinate verification.
- Values strictly evidence-backed: lat/lng NULL for 7 places (only Ayyappa = approved plus-code decode 13.279913/77.552234); price NULL for 6 (Brundavana ₹ from ₹300/2, Chickpet ₹₹ from ₹1000/2); hours only where single consistent source (Manjunatha 06:00-23:00, Halli Nanasu 11:00-23:00), NULL on conflicts (Ayyappa, Brundavana, Priyanka, Annapoorneshwari, Chickpet); phones only where published (Ayyappa +918088599999 single-source-flagged, Annapoorneshwari +918746888318 two-source agreement; Halli Nanasu NULL — 3 conflicting); ratings with platform+count in comments (4.6 Google/253, 4.2 JD/3357, 4.2 JD/263, 3.4 JD/278, 4.2 RG/615, 4.1 JD/354; 0 = not captured for Ayyappa + Chickpet); tags only evidence-supported.
- NULL-tolerance code changes: types.ts Place lat/lng/priceRange/costForTwo optional + RestaurantRow lat/lng/price_level nullable; mappers null→undefined; location.withDistances excludes coord-less places (never NaN); supabaseBackend bbox fallback filters null-coord rows (type predicate, no assertion); RatingBadge returns null at rating 0; PlaceCards + PlaceDetailScreen conditional price/distance rendering; detail shows "Coordinates pending verification".
- Verified: bun run lint clean; dev server ready; nearby (2 origins), search, category/tiffin all HTTP 200 mode "supabase", places [] (DB still empty — user has not run the INSERT).
- Nothing executed in Supabase; SQL shown to user for review.

Stage Summary:
- supabase/insert-batch-001-pilot-8.sql ready for the user to run; double-layer duplicate protection (fixed uuid + name guard), atomic transaction.
- After the user runs it: only Ayyappa (has coords) surfaces in nearby discovery; the other 7 appear automatically once coordinates are filled via the UPDATE template.

Mock route removed immediately after (unroute verified).
- Verdict = A: normal Supabase rate limiting. Real attempts consumed quotas: previous session's documented E2E (signup verify.user@foodie.app → confirmation email; duplicate-signup attempt → resend for unconfirmed user → 2nd email; unconfirmed login; bad-credentials login) + the user's own retries since. Supabase free tier without custom SMTP allows only ~2–4 confirmation emails/hour (multiple sources: dev.to "3 emails/hour on user registration", GitHub issue "429 email rate limit exceeded on Free Plan") and modest per-IP token-endpoint limits → both over_email_send_rate_limit (Sign Up) and over_request_rate_limit (Log In) map to our "Too many attempts…" message. Windows are hourly — waiting resolves it; retrying keeps hitting 429.
- No code changes made (none needed). Lint clean. No new users created (all test requests were intercepted by the local mock; zero requests reached Supabase). Dev server still healthy via the hotfix-1 wrapper tree.

Stage Summary:
- CONFIRMED A: Foodie sends exactly ONE auth request per button click and ZERO at boot — no repeated-request bug exists. The rate-limit error is genuine Supabase free-tier throttling caused by earlier E2E testing + user retries within the hourly window.
- Zero code changes; lint clean. Evidence method: Playwright-level network interception counting (no extra auth traffic created, keys untouched).
- Options for the user (their call, NOT implemented): wait ~1h for the window to reset; confirm the pending verify.user@foodie.app via dashboard magic-link; or temporarily disable "Confirm email" / configure custom SMTP in the Supabase dashboard to raise email quota for pilot testing.

Added SUPABASE_UNAVAILABLE_NOTICE ("…list is empty for now — nothing was substituted") and a supabaseFailureNotice(error, context) mapper distinguishing demo-fallback vs honest-empty notices.
- BUG FOUND + FIXED (failure states): no timeout handling — hung Supabase queries could hang API routes forever, and client fetch had no budget. Added server-side 10s QUERY_TIMEOUT_MS withQueryTimeout() wrapper on every Supabase call in serverRepository, SupabaseQueryTimeoutError, and a 15s AbortSignal.timeout on the client repository fetch (TimeoutError → friendly retryable DataAccessError).
- BUG FOUND + FIXED (classification): RLS/permission refusals were indistinguishable from generic server errors. Added classifySupabaseError() → missing-table | timeout | permission | server-error (42501/PGRST301/302, row-level-security, permission-denied messages) with dedicated SUPABASE_PERMISSION_NOTICE + SUPABASE_TIMEOUT_NOTICE; isMissingPlacesTable exported.
- FIXED (404 honesty): restaurants/[id], destinations/[id]/foods, destinations/[id]/places returned bare "not found" 404s even when the real cause was a DB failure — now carry result.notice in the body (matching /api/places/[id]).
- BUG FOUND + FIXED (UI): ReviewsSection treated a ready-but-empty demo response (honest notice, zero reviews) as an error → misleading Retry button and the "No reviews yet" state never rendered. Split loadError (fetch failed → retryable) from loadNotice (informational); empty state + notice now render without a false Retry affordance. Browser-verified fixed.
